never from the Spanish or French post.

### A7 · Italy — Italian posts rank here

Unblocked: the site publishes in Italian since July 2026. **These are
written in Italian only.**

Every row below was reached at the vendor's own domain on 30 July 2026.
None of the rows as first written published a price, and that line has since
been wrong four times: XDENT publishes a starting price, DentalOpera and Dentus
publish tariffs, and UNO publishes both a licence price and its canoni. Check
the pricing page every run rather than trusting this paragraph.

| Target | Slug | Why |
|---|---|---|
| XDENT (CGM Italia) | `dentalpin-vs-xdent` | `xdent.it`. Claims "more than 8.000 dentists" in Italy and is the only one marketing itself as native on Mac and Windows. Same parent as CGM Z1.PRO in A6, and the two posts must not repeat each other: different product, different market, different page |
| OrisDent Q (OrisLine) | `dentalpin-vs-orisdent` | `orisline.com`. Publishes a whole product family (OrisDent Q, the cloud OrisDent Air, OrisLab Q for labs). A suite sold as one is the closest thing Italy has to our module argument, so meet it head-on |
| GipoDental (GIPO) | `dentalpin-vs-gipodental` | `gipo.it`. **Checked 6 August 2026: the pricing pages exist (`/prezzi`, `/prezzi-gipodental`) and list what each plan contains, but carry no figure, only "Prezzo calcolato individualmente". So the "non pubblica i prezzi" line holds, with that precision.** Ownership: the site's legal entity is Docplanner Italy Srl, and the same group owns MioDottore, which GipoDental integrates with. Verified from their own site |
| AlfaDocs | `dentalpin-vs-alfadocs` | `alfadocs.com`. Cloud-first, positioned around the dentist-patient relationship rather than the back office. A different pitch from ours, which makes "scegli AlfaDocs se" the section that carries the post |
| Appuntoo | `dentalpin-vs-appuntoo` | `appuntoo.com`. Aims at small and medium practices and markets a free tier, so it is the one Italian row where free-versus-free has to be compared carefully and fairly |
| UNO (Dental Trey s.r.l.) | `dentalpin-vs-uno` | `dentaltrey-uno.it`, verified 28 August 2026. 30+ years of dental software, claims 8.000 professionals, cloud ("MyBusiness Cloud"). Dental Trey is first a supplies distributor; the software division is the target. The product is UNO: no product named "Evolution" exists on its site. **Checked 2 September 2026: five things this row did not know. The site sits behind a SiteGround captcha (`sg-captcha: challenge`, HTTP 202 with a 169-byte refresh stub) that defeats a plain fetch: retry with a cookie jar until a 200 comes back, it does within a handful of tries. It publishes both a licence price and canoni, so the "none of them published a price" line above is wrong for this row: licence "a partire da € 1.900 per 3 posti di lavoro" plus Start-Up da € 26,5/mese (max 4 posti), Full da € 76/mese and Full Digital da € 130/mese (posti illimitati), with 12-month financing or a 36-month BNP leasing. The asterisk in their plan table means "Attivabile come add on", and both "Fatturazione elettronica" and "Invio telematico Tessera Sanitaria" carry it on all three plans, so neither Italian obligation is included in any plan: that is what makes the post. `MyBusiness Cloud` is a cloud KPI layer over a product licensed per `posto di lavoro`, not a cloud PMS, so do not write it as cloud-native. And ownership is stated on their own Chi Siamo page, not inferred: "Nel 2015 Dental Trey si è rafforzata grazie all'entrata nel gruppo Henry Schein Inc." and the informatica division "è parte del gruppo internazionale Henry Schein ONE", the same group as Dentrix, Dentally and Software of Excellence in A3.** |
| Windent (Quaderno Elettronico S.r.l.) | `dentalpin-vs-windent` | `quadernoelettronico.it`, verified 28 August 2026. Desktop with mobile access, claims 20.000+ professionals. Aggregators attribute it to Zucchetti while its own site declares Quaderno Elettronico S.r.l.: state ownership only as its own site does. **Checked 3 September 2026: the ownership guard held, no group is named anywhere on their own pages, so nothing was repeated from elsewhere. Five things the row did not know. It publishes prices, so the "none of them published a price" line above is wrong here too: acquisto "a partire da 750€" (with the first fiscal year of fatturazione elettronica up to 1000 documents, Sistema TS, 6 hours of consulenza and 100 SMS credits) plus assistenza "a partire da 200€ /anno" or "a partire da € 50 /mese", two figures that do not reconcile and are both "a partire da". The Italian compliance set is their strong side and is complete: Sistema TS, SDI with ten-year conservazione, and a dedicated Referto FSE 2.0 service whose page states the 31 March 2026 obligation. "Quaderno Online" is remote access to the installed program on their server, not a browser-native cloud PMS: do not write it as cloud. MIA is a paid-looking AI telephone assistant with no published price, like most of the 39 servizi. And no page consulted publishes system requirements, so Windows-only cannot be stated from their site; the published post says so rather than guessing.** |
| DentalOpera (MedicalOpera srl) | `dentalpin-vs-dentalopera` | `dentalopera.it`, verified 28 August 2026. Founded 2017, MedicalOpera srl, Cervignano del Friuli (UD). **Publishes prices: da 88 €/mese on its home page and on `/prezzi/`, so the usual "non pubblica i prezzi" line is wrong here**. **Checked 4 September 2026: two corrections to this row. "100% cloud" is not supported by their own site: the word "cloud" appears on none of the pages consulted, there are no system requirements and no hosting or data location anywhere, so the published post describes it as an account-based gestionale (login on `gest.dentalopera.it`) rather than asserting an architecture the vendor does not state. And the 88 € is a floor, not a price: the same page carries "Nessun modulo nascosto, nessun costo extra per utente" above a MODULI AGGIUNTIVI block of eight unpriced modules, two of which are "Poltrone operative" and "Sedi", and closes with "non abbiamo un listino fisso". Three more things the row did not know. Sistema TS is covered ("Sistema MEF TS: Invio telematico dei dati al Sistema Tessera Sanitaria e MEF") but sits in the same feature list as gestione magazzino, which `/prezzi/` sells as a paid add-on, so whether TS filing is inside the 88 € cannot be told from their pages: the post states both and cites each. Fatturazione elettronica illimitata *is* unambiguously in the base price and FSE is unambiguously an add-on. Clinical depth is real (odontogramma grafico, cartella parodontale, cartella ortodontica, prestazioni per arcata, gestione per riuniti), the only install-base figure is "Già scelto da 600+ studi" on `/agenda-governata/`, onboarding is free, and cancellation is "senza necessità di preavviso".** |
| Dentus (jErgoSoft Srl) | `dentalpin-vs-dentus` | `dentus.it`, verified 28 August 2026. Desktop since 2000, claims 15.000+ clients. **Publishes prices: 33,20-135 €/mese (`/it/prezzi-software-dentale/`)**. **Checked 5 September 2026: the price range is right, and five things the row did not know. All four plans (Facile 41,50 €, Standard 62,50 €, Premium 121 €, Elite 135 €, each 20% less billed annually) carry the word "Monoutenza", and "Collegamento in rete" is a separate module priced nowhere on the site ("la versione limitata a 4 computer, a 8 e licenze aggiuntive"), so a practice with more than one workstation cannot compute its cost from the published tariff: that is what makes the post. The Premium plan does not list the Modulo Parodontale that the cheaper Standard includes, and Modulo MEF730 (the Sistema TS filing) appears only on Premium and Elite. The agenda is itself an add-on module and is absent from the base plan. Fatturazione elettronica is described as a full service ("Facciamo tutto noi: Ricevimento, Spedizione e Conservazione") but appears in no plan row, so whether it is included cannot be told from their pages; the published post says so. And the deployment story is unusually close to ours and was conceded in full: published requirements are "Windows™ 11, 10, 8, 7, XP oppure con MacOsX™ oppure con Linux" via Java, the database goes "Sul tuo PC della rete locale, o sul tuo NAS o sul sito", and the home page states "I Dati che introduci sono sempre di tua proprietà". No VAT note and no contract length anywhere. The word odontogramma appears nowhere; they write "schema dentale". Two more they publish and nobody else does: the ASL di Torino "progetto Castoro" from 2005, and a cartella parodontale "sviluppata con esperti della Dental School di Torino" on 4 or 6 sites.** |
| FELG Dent | `dentalpin-vs-felg-dent-italia` | `felgdent.com/it/`, verified 17 September 2026. The "flaky Italian product site" note below is stale: it fetches cleanly and has its own Italian pages and a `/it/listino/`. The publisher is **Polish**, FELG Software Sp. z o.o., selling into Italy: state the origin plainly rather than leaving it for the reader to find, the way the Aerona row does for its French owner, and do not treat it as a weakness. Claims "Software cloud per il tuo studio dentistico", "oltre 16.000 dentisti" and "Oltre 4000 studi si sono affidati a noi" (**the 16.000 is dentists and hygienists, not studi**, per their own Polish wording), "oltre 10 anni di esperienza", ISO 27001, and "30 giorni di prova, zero obblighi". Check whether `/it/listino/` carries euro figures the run this is written. **What decides this post is Italian compliance**: establish from their Italian pages whether fatturazione elettronica and the Sistema Tessera Sanitaria filing are covered, because the same product's Polish site leads on KSeF and NFZ, which are not Italian obligations, and a feature list translated across a border is exactly what this section has caught before. **Do not give this post the same filename as the A8 FELG Dent row**: posts sharing a filename across directories are emitted as hreflang alternates of each other, and these are two different posts for two different markets, not translations. **Written 20 September 2026. What the run established: every Italian page fetches cleanly with a browser user agent, the stale-note in this row was right. `/it/listino/` carries euro figures, so the "check whether" is settled: SOLO 29 €, START 39 €, FLEX 59 €, MAX 79 €, all "canone mensile netto*" where the asterisk is defined as "*calcolo delle media mensile". The page offers four durations as tabs (Mensile, Annuale, Biennale, Triennale) but only one set of figures is in the served HTML and the others need JavaScript, so **which duration the 29 € belongs to cannot be determined** and the post says so rather than guessing. That matters because the licence agreement bars early exit on prepaid periods ("senza possibilità di un recesso anticipato"), against 30 days' notice on the monthly. The unit is per medico o igienista, not per riunito. The Italian compliance question this row asked is answered and the answer is split: **Sistema TS yes**, quoted from the home page ("FELG Dent consente di inviare le fatture direttamente al Sistema TS, oppure di generare i file — in diversi formati — per lo studio di contabilità"), **SDI no** (the words SDI, Sistema di Interscambio, fattura elettronica and fatturazione elettronica appear on no page consulted, and nothing addresses conservazione a norma). What makes the post is that the localisation is half-done and it is visible on their own pages: three of the thirteen sections on `/it/funzioni/` are in Polish and describe Polish obligations (Rejestr P1, e-Recepty, e-Skierowania, eWUŚ, Rozliczenia NFZ, Przedpłaty za wizyty), the Italian listino sells two configurations priced in złoty ("NFZ Konfiguracja PLN 500", "Certyfikaty P1 konfiguracja PLN 179"), "P1" is listed among the modules every subscription includes, the Italian home page repeats its departments block in Polish and carries "Dodaj tu swój tekst nagłówka" plus Lorem ipsum, four of five client stories on `/it/funzioni/` are Lorem ipsum, and the English API page still shows the template line "No fake quotes or photos!". Handle that as fact and quotation only, never as mockery: the no-adjectives rule is what makes it land. Two things to concede generously, because they are real and documented. The Italian connection is founding, not marketing: `chisiamo` dates the project to 2014 and credits "Guido Pasquantonio, dentista e docente di odontoiatria presso l'Università degli Studi di Roma Tor Vergata", with "circa trenta appassionati tra Polonia e Italia", market launch 2018, plus Italian LinkedIn/Facebook/Instagram/YouTube channels and a Milan phone line, +39 02 21119068. And the parodontal module is deep and documented in Italian: eight parameters per tooth, vestibular versus lingual/palatal, mean CAL, mean pocket depth, plaque and BOP percentages, comparison against previous exams. Entity published plainly on `/it/contatti/`: FELG Software Sp. z o.o., P.IVA 6762479659, VAT EU PL6762479659, KRS 0000520292, Sławkowska 25/9 and ul. Pijarska 9/7, Cracovia. FELG API is genuinely documented (`api.felg.app/docs/` serves a Swagger UI) but gated and priced: START/FLEX/MAX only, 12 €/month in the euro zone, 1000 requests included, 0,01 € each after, max 100 records per request. FAQ states all modules are in every plan, while the listino prices RX 49 €, VoIP 199 €, SMS packs 24-180 € and extra storage from 9 €/month: both are published, carry both. Exit terms are published and decent: 30 days to download patient data from the account after termination, though **no export format is named anywhere**, and importing files out needs the "FELG Box" gateway, unpriced. **Where patient data physically sits is published nowhere** (no country, no region, no data centre, no hosting provider on any page consulted), ISO 27001 is claimed with no certificate number, and the Italian *Informativa sulla privacy* is founded on the Polish pre-GDPR statute ("legge del 29 agosto 1997") naming the "Ispettore Generale per la Protezione dei Dati Personali", the authority replaced in 2018, while the home page advertises GDPR and the FAQ cites NIS 2. Contract and invoicing are Polish: fattura NP with reverse charge, Italian clinic self-accounts IVA.** |
| OpenStudio (OPEN S.R.L.) | `dentalpin-vs-openstudio` | `openstudio.cloud/gestionale-studio-dentistico`, verified 24 September 2026. Italy, cloud, dental, **OPEN S.R.L., P.IVA 12179040964**, "realizzato in Italia". **The only row in this section with a genuinely free tier published on its own page, and that is what the post is about: Piano Standard €0/mese, capped at 30 pazienti and mono-utente; Piano Professional €35/mese; Piano Business €25/mese per utente from 2 users, all "esclusi di IVA" and billed "annuale anticipata" after the trial.** A 30-patient cap is not a free product for a working studio and saying exactly where the cap bites is the whole comparison, made in their own numbers and without an adjective. **This row also belongs in the `software-gestionale-dentisti-gratis` post**, the way DASI does for Spain, and that post should be revisited the run this one is written. Settle from their own pages: whether Sistema Tessera Sanitaria and fatturazione elettronica are in the free tier or only above it |
| DrawDent Desktop (il-software.it) | `dentalpin-vs-drawdent` | `il-software.it/software_dentisti.htm`, verified 24 September 2026. Italy, dental, **desktop Windows with local data storage**, **P.IVA 05975660878**. **Publishes prices and they are perpetual, not monthly: €79 base "senza abbonamento", €99,00, €119,00 and €129,00 for the higher versions, "licenza perpetua", "pagamento unico", "nessun rinnovo automatico della licenza".** This is the clearest licence-shape comparison in any market queue — a one-off €79 against a subscription — and the post is worth writing precisely because the honest answer for a single-chair studio that wants no recurring bill may well be theirs. **Never annualise their figure or set it against a monthly one in the same table cell**; put licence model in its own row as a fact, not a chip, per *Making a post look designed*. Settle from their pages what a perpetual licence excludes: Sistema Tessera Sanitaria filing, fatturazione elettronica via SDI, updates when a tracciato changes, backups and multi-postazione are the questions a desktop perpetual product has to answer in Italy |
| Horizon Blue (Caes Software s.r.l.) | `dentalpin-vs-horizon-blue` | `caes.it/horizon`, verified 24 September 2026, **written 27 September 2026**. Italy, dental, **desktop Windows only** ("il software lavora solo su sistemi operativi Windows", and on a Mac "occorre necessariamente un emulatore di Windows"), **Caes Software s.r.l., P.IVA IT08298111009**, product line dated "dal 1991 ad oggi" with the site copyright running from 1989, both rows they win. **Checked 27 September 2026: the four-level listino is confirmed and the row was short by two levels. Full tariff: Visualizzatore con Agenda € 98,00/anno, BASIC (3 moduli a scelta) € 197,00/anno, PREMIUM (5 moduli a scelta) € 257,00/anno, TOP (tutti i moduli) € 317,00/anno, each paid level plus a € 93,00 first-year activation only, with monthly card renewal at € 8,90 / € 18,10 / € 23,60 / € 29,10. **All of it is "IVA 22% INCLUSA", which nearly every other Italian row quotes net, so never put these figures next to a competitor's without saying so.** Four more things the row did not know. **The multiplier is the per-workstation licence**, published twice: "In caso di uso contemporaneo su più computer è necessario sottoscrivere più abbonamenti" on the listino and "Ogni computer su cui è utilizzato un prodotto della Caes Software deve avere una sua licenza" in the FAQ; whether the optional services are per studio or per postazione is published nowhere, and the post says so rather than computing it. **Sistema TS is a paid annual add-on at € 93,00/anno**, and the SDI service runs € 61,00 (XML only) to € 485,00/anno (unlimited sends), with Caes itself "un'azienda accredita per la trasmissione presso l'Agenzia delle Entrate"; ricette bianche/certificati INPS € 122,00-549,00 by prescriber count, WhatsApp Desktop € 96,00/anno, Modulistica PRO € 146,00/anno, UDI € 73,00/anno, SMS packs € 77,00-976,00 with no expiry, tessera sanitaria readers € 122,00/€ 183,00. Because BASIC gives only three of the selectable modules, a studio wanting agenda, cartella odontoiatrica, contabile, immagini and parodontale is on PREMIUM by design: the published post works that to € 628,00 first year and € 535,00 on renewal for one postazione with Sistema TS and 250 e-invoices, labelled as arithmetic on their figures. **They publish two versions of what happens at expiry** and the post carries both: "il software restrà in sola lettura per un ulteriore anno" on the Horizon page against "la lettura dei dati inseriti è sempre possibile" in the FAQ. Two figures on the page are **commented out of the HTML and must not be cited**: the Magazzino module (sold separately as Stack) and a € 610,00 CRM integration; that is why their prose says "nove moduli" while eight are listed. Rows they win outright: Sistema TS, SDI transmission, ricette bianche elettroniche, UDI register with passaporto implantare, tessera sanitaria reader, Asterisk centralino integration, Italian interface, phone support by their own technicians, no server to run, and quarterly or half-yearly instalments "senza nessun costo aggiuntivo". Their cartella parodontale is real, not a facade (tasche, recessioni, forcazioni, mobilità, indice di placca, sanguinamento with graphs), so **do not reuse the "parodontogramma missing" angle** from the DrawDent post here. **No install-base figure appears anywhere**: the home page counters for "Anni di Esperienza" and "Clienti" render empty, and the post says that rather than guessing. No cloud or web version of Horizon Blue appears on the pages consulted, and no full-archive export is documented, only riepiloghi "in diversi formati (excel, testo, xml e html)". Caes sells a separate product per specialty (Studio D entry-level dental, Studio V veterinary, Horizon ME aesthetic medicine, Studio Fe, FastCeph, Stack Logic), so the "polispecialistiche" in their own headline is the page heading and not a multi-vertical single product: state it that way**
| — | `software-gestionale-dentisti-gratis` | Head term. Native post naming Italian vendors |
| — | `alternativa-open-source-a-xdent` | Highest-intent Italian query in the space |

Checked 28 August 2026 and **not** added: EasyDent (`easydent.it` does
not resolve; the EasyDent that exists is American), and no Italian PMS
named Julia exists (the real Software Dental Julia is Spanish and sits in
A1).

Checked 24 September 2026 and **not** added: **MedicHub**
(`medichub.it/gestionale-odontoiatrico`). It is a real Italian dental
cloud PMS, P.IVA 17916741006, with native Sistema Tessera Sanitaria
integration and invio automatico al MEF, and it ranks well for the
Italian head term — and its own page carries the sentence that keeps it
out: **"Da agosto 2026 MedicHub non è più in vendita."** A product
withdrawn from sale is not a comparison target: the reader choosing today
cannot buy it, and the post would be stale the day it shipped. This is
the case *Where the routine publishes and flags* describes, caught before
a post existed rather than after, which is the whole argument for
verifying a queue row at the vendor's own site the day it is added. **Do
not re-add from an aggregator listing** — several still list it as
current. It comes back only if their own site says it is for sale again.

**Sistema Tessera Sanitaria is the Italian compliance moat.** Every
practice in Italy files patient spending to the STS, and Italian vendors
advertise it as a feature. Establish what Dentalpin does and does not do
there before the first Italian comparison goes out, and say it plainly
either way. It belongs in Queue B as its own Italian post.

### A8 · Poland — Polish posts rank here

Unblocked 28 August 2026: every row below was reached at the vendor's own
domain that day, which is what the rotation was waiting for. **These are
written in Polish only.** One market quirk decides the shape of these
posts: most Polish vendors publish prices, so the "does not publish
prices" line that carries other markets is mostly unavailable here, and
the comparison has to win on deployment, data ownership and openness
instead. Prices go in złoty.

| IQ Dental | `dentalpin-vs-iq-dental` | `iqdental.pl`, verified 20 September 2026. Cloud and says so, "nowoczesne oprogramowanie do gabinetu stomatologicznego stworzone w modelu SaaS", "Przyszłość stomatologii jest w chmurze", so it is the closest Polish analogue to the cloud-versus-your-server argument, the mirror of the SmartDental row. **Publishes a full tariff in złoty** (`/cennik/`), and the shape is the post: two products priced separately, IQ Dental FULL at 127 to 708 zł brutto per user per month and IQ Dental EDM at 84 to 480 zł, scaled by user count from 1 to 12+ and by a one, three or five-year term, with "Pacjenci, personel rejestracji i asystentki otrzymują konta za darmo". Brutto, not netto, which is the opposite of most of this section: check the label the run this is written. And **no spółka, NIP, KRS or address appeared on the pages consulted** including the pricing page footer, so establish the entity from the regulamin or polityka prywatności and if none is published say exactly that, the way the Dendoo post does. The footer does link an "Umowa licencji/powierzenia", which is the art. 28 processor contract and the natural internal link to the new `umowa-powierzenia-przetwarzania-danych-w-gabinecie-stomatologicznym` row in Queue B. Install base claimed as "6 500 dentystów" and "1 500 obsłużonych gabinetów": two different units, quote both with their units. **Written 21 September 2026, and both of the things this row said to establish resolved the other way.** The entity is published, in the regulamin and again in the licence agreement: IQ Dental System Spółka z ograniczoną odpowiedzialnością, Wrocław (52-326), ul. Kwiatkowskiego 4, KRS 0000646702, NIP 8672242363, REGON 365847216, biuro@iqdental.pl, with a phone (71 308 41 45) and the correspondence address on `/kontakt/`. And **P1 is named**, twice: "Integracja P1" sits in the FULL-versus-EDM table on `/rozwiazania/oprogramowanie/` and is ticked for *both* versions, while `/proces-wdrozenia/` publishes help configuring "Platforma P1"; §8¹ of the licence goes further and describes support for reporting zdarzenia medyczne to SIM with ICD-9 and ICD-10 code suggestions, at the licensee's risk. e-Recepta, e-skierowanie, eWUŚ, NFZ and KSeF appeared on none of the twenty pages consulted, and neither did parodontogram or periodontogram, so the post scopes each of those. Five more things the row did not know. The tariff is **not per user**: it is a tiered total for the whole practice (FULL annual 127, 206, 255, 304, 355, 408, 455, 504, 558, 610, 660, 708 zł brutto for 1 to 12 users, EDM 84 to 480 zł), so "per user per month" above is wrong. The price is published **two ways**: the cennik labels every figure brutto, while §6 of the licence says the Cennik figures are netto with VAT added, a 23 % gap between two of the vendor's own documents. The trial is published two ways as well, 14 days on the home page, the cennik and the licence definition against "Wypróbuj za darmo przez 30 dni" on the backups, migration and diagram pages, and support is too (home page helpdesk "online jak i pod telefonem" against §11 "wyłącznie za pośrednictwem adresu e-mail"). What makes the post is the vendor's own FULL-versus-EDM table: the cheap EDM tier has **no plany leczenia, no statystyki module at all, no pulpit, no animacje 3D, no zadania, no powiadomienia, no poczekalnia and no wyszukiwanie duplikatów**, and its karta pacjenta is annotated "(uproszczona)", so a practice that plans treatment is on FULL by design. Rows they win outright: P1 in both tiers, their **own** ISO/IEC 27001:2013 since 2020 via LL-C (correctly separated on `/bezpieczenstwo-danych/certyfikaty-bezpieczenstwa/` from the partner certificates they list as their partners'), backups four times a day at 00:05, 11:00, 14:00 and 17:00 with a stated 3-hour worst case, AWS plus OVHcloud with **Warsaw named** (the AWS region is not), free migration, implementation, training and devices, free accounts for patients, reception and assistants, Carestream and Vatech imaging, Znany Lekarz (FULL only), and a published art. 28 processor contract with a real audit right (14 working days' notice) and 24-hour breach notification. The counterweights are theirs too and are all in the licence: fixed one, three or five-year term, non-refundable fees, no refund on downgrade, liability capped at 2× the period's fee, rękojmia excluded, no uptime figure or SLA anywhere, no source code and an auto-terminating licence on any reverse-engineering attempt, and the licensor may assign the contract to a third party without the customer's consent. Exit is good and the post concedes it: 60 days' read and download after termination, confirmed by a protocol whose template is an annex, then deletion after 90 days. No API documentation appears on any page consulted. The honest verdict for a Polish practice reporting to P1 is that they fit better today, and the published post says so |

Availability is published without an SLA: the home page counter's `data-target` is 99.99 for "Ciągłości pracy systemu" (it renders 0 % without JavaScript, so read the attribute) and the FAQ attributes "dostępność na poziomie 99.99%" to Azure, while the word SLA appears only as a label ("96% Zgłoszeń rozwiązanych w SLA", "Błyskawiczna reakcja (SLA)"). Export is partial and published: raporty to XLSX and PDF, faktury to XML (JPK) and XLSX, and **no full record export anywhere**; the archiwizacja service holds data "przez co najmniej 20 lat" instead. | Gabinet Stomatologiczny (gabinet2.pl) | `dentalpin-vs-gabinet-stomatologiczny` | `gabinet2.pl`, verified 20 September 2026. Desktop Windows and the only row in this section that publishes a **perpetual licence tariff** rather than a subscription, which is the SmartDental argument taken further and is the comparison to write honestly. Its own pages name "Program działa na Windows 11/10/8/7/VISTA w wersjach 32 i 64 bitowych", so Windows-only is a fact here and not a guess, and it publishes "raportowanie zdarzeń medycznych do systemu P1" and "wystawianie e-recepty". The tariff is on `zamowienie.html`, an order form rather than a pricing page, and it is concrete: Gabinet stomatologiczny 21.0 pojedyncza licencja 800,00 zł, wersja serwerowa (3 stanowiska sieciowe / dostęp przez internet / VPN) 1 200,00 zł, aktualizacja or kolejne stanowisko 400,00 zł, zdalna pomoc with the P1 certificates 100,00 zł, a dedicated SMS modem 100,00 zł, and the Cefalometria 2.0 module 600,00 zł bundled or 300,00 zł alone, all **netto with 23% VAT added on the form**. Two mechanics to carry rather than rediscover: the page is **windows-1250 encoded**, so decode it before quoting or every ł and ę comes out mangled, and the form itself is the price list, so re-read it the run this is written. The only contact published is a phone number, "tel. 601 472 783", and **no company name, NIP or address appeared on the pages consulted**: establish the entity or say plainly that none is published. One tell worth handling as fact and not as mockery: the order form still bases its consent language on "ustawa o ochronie danych osobowych z dnia 29 sierpnia 1997r. (Dz.U. nr 133 z 1997r.)", the statute replaced in 2018, the same thing the FELG Dent Italia post found in that vendor's Italian privacy notice. Quote it, do not editorialise on it, and it is also the natural link to the new Queue B processor-contract row. **Written 22 September 2026. What the run established. Encoding first: the "windows-1250" in this row is wrong. Every page declares `charset=iso-8859-2` and decodes correctly as **ISO-8859-2**; decoding as windows-1250 turns ą into ± on every page, not just the order form. A plain fetch with a browser user agent serves all nine pages HTTP 200 and no interstitial. The site is nine pages (index, moduly, demo, faq, zamowienie, historia, p1, erecepty, sms), all reachable from the menu or from body links, and `sitemap.xml` is 404, so completeness cannot be asserted. The tariff on `zamowienie.html` is unchanged from this row and the run confirmed one figure it did not carry: aktualizacja Cefalometrii do wersji 2.0 at 150,00 zł netto. What makes the post is **e-Recepta, and it is not what the row assumed**: `erecepty.html` says the program "został zintegrowany z aplikacją gabinet.gov.pl", meaning the e-Recepta button carries the user out to the government portal (with automatic login and a right-click helper for the PESEL), so prescribing happens on the portal and not in the program. P1 is the real integration and is the strongest thing they publish: certificates from rpwdl.ezdrowie.gov.pl, a [Test] button, "automatyczną wysyłkę zdarzenia medycznego po zapisie każdej wizyty" plus a bulk end-of-day send. The second centrepiece is in the FAQ and is the most generous exit-and-entry term in the Polish section: **"Nasza firma wykonuje bezpłatnie przeniesienie danych z każdego programu komputerowego"**, with import from XLS, XML "etc.", and it is what the post concedes outright. Licence terms are published in the FAQ too: updates are not compulsory, the program runs without a time limit, and "Nowy użytkownik otrzymuje wszystkie aktualizacje przez okres jednego roku od daty zakupu", after which a further release is the 400 zł netto per-workstation line. Architecture is a shared Windows folder: program and database on the main computer, the folder shared, the network workstation pointed at it; the wersja serwerowa adds Internet/VPN access to the central base; moving machines is "przegranie całego katalogu". Install base and dates from `historia.html`: "Program powstał w 1996 roku", "ponad 500 gabinetów w całej Polsce", versions 1.0 (1996), 2.0 (2003), 3.0 (2013) and **21 (2021), which is the newest release**. The entity question resolved the way the row feared: the only contact anywhere is info@gabinet2.pl and tel. 601 472 783, and **no company name, NIP, KRS or address appears on any of the nine pages**, so the post says exactly that and nothing more. The words **parodontogram, periodontogram, kosztorys, "plan leczenia", kopia zapasowa, backup, eksport, API, chmura, NFZ, eWUŚ, e-skierowanie, KSeF, RODO, regulamin, polityka prywatności, szyfrowanie and SLA appear in the rendered text of none of the nine pages**, so the post scopes each of them to "konsultowane strony" rather than asserting absence from the product. One caution for a re-verification run: `index.html` carries a commented-out list item, `<!--li>program nie obsługuje rozliczeń z NFZ.</li-->`, which does not render and was **not** used, the same treatment the Tactis row gives its hidden fourteenth name. Two smaller tells worth carrying: the footer copyright year differs per page (1996-2021 on six pages, 1996-2022 on index and erecepty, **1996-2025 on zamowienie**, which makes the order form the freshest page and the right source for prices), and the SMS module uses the clinic's own SIM in any GSM device as a USB modem (Huawei E3131 named), so SMS runs at the practice's own carrier rate with no per-message vendor charge, which is a row they win. The 1997 data-protection statute in the order-form consent is still there and was quoted without comment, as this row instructs |
| CALDIS | `dentalpin-vs-caldis` | `caldis.pl/program-dla-stomatologa`, verified 24 September 2026. Poland, cloud, with Android and iOS apps, and a dedicated dental page. **Publishes prices in złoty on `caldis.pl/cennik`: Plan Standardowy 39,00 zł/mc rocznie or 49,00 zł/mc miesięcznie, Plan Solo and Plan Premium 49,00 / 59,00 zł/mc, Plan Premium Plus 89,00 / 99,00 zł/mc.** That is the cheapest published tariff in the Polish section, well under the ProDentis per-fotel licence, and the post is about what that buys. **The thing the post must not get wrong: those plans are not dental plans.** The cennik prices one product across professions ("kalendarz medyczny dla lekarza, fizjoterapeuty") and the stomatology page is an application area, so this is a multi-vertical medical system with a dental page, the way Klinikare and Nubimed are in A1 — state that plainly and establish from their own pages whether a diagram zębowy and a periodontogram exist at all, and in which plan, before the table claims either. No legal entity is named on the product or pricing pages consulted, only the address `pomoc@caldis.pl`: look for a regulamin or polityka prywatności carrying the spółka and NIP, and if none exists, say what the pages do and do not publish rather than asserting anything. **Written 26 September 2026. What the run established.** The entity question resolved the opposite way to what this row feared: it **is** published, in the regulamin, the regulamin aplikacji dla firm, the polityka prywatności and on `/kontakt`, and it is a **sole trader, not a spółka**: Mateusz Stwora, trading as PELMO GROUP MATEUSZ STWORA, ul. Krakowska 18A, 43-340 Kozy, CEIDG (so no KRS), NIP 9372598168, REGON 388892503, biuro@caldis.pl, tel. 691 803 384. State that neutrally as a fact about scale, the way the Dentasys row in A1 does. The tariff figures in this row are confirmed exactly, and two things it did not carry: **every figure is netto** ("powyższe ceny są cenami netto"), and the plan order on the page is Standardowy, Premium, Solo, Premium Plus. **What makes the post is the plan gate, and it inverts the cheap headline**: Plan Standardowy at 39 zł has **no rezerwacje online, no powiadomienia SMS, no płatności online/BLIK, no CALDIS klient app, no portal klienta, no interactive documents with e-signature, no vouchers and no extended invoicing**; Premium at 49 zł adds SMS only; the full set arrives in Solo (capped at **1 kalendarz and 1 użytkownik**) and Premium Plus, so a two-dentist practice wanting online booking starts at **89 zł netto/mc annually, not 39**, and wsparcie wielu lokalizacji is Premium Plus only at +39 zł netto/mc per site. Extras that move the bill: SMS 0,08 zł netto (PL) / 0,49 zł (foreign) billed in prepaid **Punkty CALDIS**, nadpisana nazwa nadawcy 9,90 zł netto/mc, 1 GB storage included then 3 zł netto/mc per GB, widget 99 zł netto/mc (Solo and Premium Plus only). The price is published **two ways and they do not reconcile**: the cennik says netto, while pkt IX of the regulamin aplikacji dla firm says "Ceny Planów podawane są w złotych polskich i zawierają wszystkie składniki, w tym podatek VAT", a 23 % gap between two of the vendor's own documents, the same shape as the IQ Dental finding. **The strongest thing on the page is pkt XIV of the regulamin aplikacji dla firm**: powierzenie runs through Załącznik nr 4 whose Załącznik nr 1 is a "zamknięty katalog powierzanych rodzajów danych osobowych", and "Powierzanie Usługodawcy do przetwarzania danych osobowych szczególnych kategorii, o których mowa w art. 9 RODO, odbywa się jedynie w związku z zawarciem uprzednio odrębnego porozumienia" — health data is art. 9 data, so by their own terms the standard DPA does not cover a patient record. Quote it, do not editorialise on it. **The dental depth question resolved against the product**: `diagram zębowy`, `parodontogram`, `periodontogram`, `dokumentacja medyczna`, `plan leczenia`, `kosztorys`, `e-recepta`, `e-skierowanie`, `P1`, `eWUŚ`, `KSeF` and `RTG` appear on **none** of the nine pages consulted, and NFZ appears once, only as a visit-category example ("pacjenta (NFZ, prywatny)"), not an integration. Also note `/program-dla-stomatologa` is **a blog article**, not a product page (breadcrumb BLOG, "wróć do artykułów"), and the cennik's own feature rows include **"karta pojazdu, urządzenia, roweru"** and **"limity kilometrów (pojazdy)"**, which is the plainest available evidence that one product serves every vertical. `caldis.pl/sitemap.xml` is **404**, so completeness cannot be asserted and the post scopes every absence to "konsultowane strony". Nothing consulted publishes an SLA or uptime figure, a hosting provider or data location, a backup policy, an export path, an install-base figure or a founding year; security is one line, "szyfrowanej transmisji danych (SSL)", about the website. Rows they win outright: the published entry price, rezerwacje online with a portal klienta and widget, **Android and iOS apps for both the practice and the patient**, Przelewy24 and BLIK, the karnety module, own document templates signed on a tablet, two-way SMS, a 30-day trial whose data carries into the paid account, and Polish interface and support. **Scope caveat worth recording:** this vendor is a booking calendar with no clinical dental record at all, which is the same test A2 used to keep Agenda Clínica out of the queue. The post was written because the row exists and the honest finding is the post, but do not add further vendors of this shape. |
| MedEQ | `dentalpin-vs-medeq` | `medeq.pl`, verified 2 October 2026. Poland, cloud, **hosted in Poland inside the EU** ("Polski hosting (UE)"), which is the data-residency argument we usually make and here is theirs. **Publishes prices in złoty net**, as most of this section does: Start 199 zł/mc (1 location, 3 users of which 2 specialists), Pro 399 zł/mc (2 locations, 10 users of which 3 specialists), Klinika 699 zł/mc (3 locations, 25 users of which 8 specialists), plus e-consultations at 99-199 zł/mc and one-off SMS packs at 49-249 zł, with 23% VAT on top and a 30-day trial without a card. **The modules are the interesting part and several have no counterpart on our side**: equipment documentation with QR codes, medical-waste tracking against BDO, sterilisation cycle records, an access audit log, staff management and invoice capture by AI OCR. BDO reporting and sterilisation traceability are statutory Polish housekeeping, not extras, so the honest table here has green chips on their side and the post wins on openness and export instead. The entity is named more completely than anywhere else in this section: **Bartosz Krzyżaniak, NIP 5542905852, REGON 382177395, ul. Sportowa 20, 88-420 Rogowo**, a sole trader rather than a company, which is a fair and non-denigrating fact to put beside our own size **Written 4 October 2026. What the run established.** Fetch mechanics: all 19 pages served HTTP 200 to a plain fetch with a browser user agent; `sitemap.xml` returns HTTP 200 and lists 81 URLs. **There is no `/cennik` page**: that path 301s to `medeq.pl/#cennik`, a section on the home page, so the tariff is the home page and a nonsense path does return a real 404. Every price in this row is confirmed exactly (Start 199, Pro 399, Klinika 699 zł netto, +249 zł per extra location, e-wizyty 99/149/199, SMS packs 49/99/249), plus three things the row did not carry: **additional seats are priced separately and prominently, 69/59/49 zł per extra user by plan and 99 zł per extra specialist in every plan**, annual payment is about 15 % off, and the pages do not say whether an extra specialist also counts as an extra user, which the post scopes rather than deriving. **The row's framing was wrong in the direction that mattered**: this is not a compliance add-on with thin dental depth. `/dla/stomatologa` publishes karta zęba in FDI notation with M/O/D/B/L surfaces and a deciduous mode, a periodontogram with 6 points per tooth (DB/B/MB/ML/L/DL), BOP, max pocket, counts ≥4/≥6 mm, % BOP and history over time, plan leczenia with kosztorys and a recorded patient-acceptance date, own cennik zabiegów, dental imaging order types (pantomogram/OPG, RVG, skrzydłówki, cefalometria, CBCT) with upload and preview, recall, pre-visit questionnaires and tablet-signed consents. So the clinical rows are a draw, not ours, and the post says so. **Export is theirs too**, which the row assumed would be our win: `/bezpieczenstwo` publishes "Eksport pełnych danych jednym kliknięciem. Brak vendor lock-in" and the home FAQ specifies a ZIP of JSON files plus attachments. Our remaining wins are price, open source, direct PostgreSQL access and a documented REST API with generated OpenAPI (theirs is "Webhook & REST API" from plan Pro with no public documentation on any page consulted). **Three things the vendor publishes two ways**, all carried in the post and sourced separately: hosting location three different ways ("Polski hosting (UE)" and "Serwery w polskim data center" on every page, "UE (Niemcy/Polska, zależnie od regionu)" in the home FAQ, "UE (OVHcloud, Polska i Francja)" on `/bezpieczenstwo` and the subprocessor list); **KSeF is not shipped** ("BDO i KSeF gotowe" and "e-faktury wbudowane" on the home page against "integrację KSeF prowadzimy jako moduł... pokażemy status" in the KSeF FAQ, which also puts *sales* invoicing at "na życzenie, w cenie planu Pro/Klinika"); and ZnanyLekarz is advertised as an "Integracja" on the Pro card but is "import wizyt z plików CSV" in the FAQ. One further contractual rozjazd: the post-termination download window is **15 days** in DPA §8 against **30 days** in EULA §6. **The strongest things they publish**, and the post concedes them: BDO with real API integration (KEO created automatically in the government system on each pickup, KPO from one screen, recipient looked up by NIP), sterilisation cycle records tied to the equipment passport, equipment passports with QR and deadline alerts, kadry with ZUS payroll, an append-only audit log enforced at database level that logs reads too, approved clinical entries sealed with a SHA-256 checksum, AES-256 at rest with a per-clinic rotating key, RCPD and DPA generated from the app, 20-year retention per art. 29, free migration done by the vendor, no setup fee, price grandfathering ("Konta założone przed zmianami cennika zachowują swoje dotychczasowe warunki bezterminowo"), an indefinite-term contract cancellable any time with no commitment period, and a **contractual** 14-day no-reason refund for every customer on top of the statutory right. **Unusually candid in two places worth crediting**: `/zaufanie` states outright that they work to the ISO/IEC 27001:2022 framework but hold no accredited certificate, publishes a self-assessment with three "częściowe" and two "planowane" statuses (external pentests among the planned), and `/funkcje/bdo` has an "Uczciwie: co robisz Ty, a co druga strona" section. **What is absent across all 19 pages**: any install-base figure, founding year or other measure of scale (there are no JS counters either, no data-target attributes, so nothing was missed the way the Medyc row warns), any uptime figure or SLA (the word SLA appears exactly once, on the Klinika plan card, while regulamin §11 is best-efforts with liability capped at one month's fees), public API documentation, and any Android or iOS app (the EULA §2 does mention "aplikacji mobilnej" and the home FAQ calls the product a PWA with no full offline support). The entity in this row is confirmed from the footer of every page. **They also publish their own comparison pages** (`/alternatywa`) against Estomed, Proassist, Prodentis, Mediporta, Dr100, Medfile, KS-SOMED and MEDchart, and **Dentalpin is named on none of the 19 pages consulted**. They state repeatedly and by design that they do not do e-recepta, e-skierowanie, P1 zdarzenia medyczne or NFZ, so that table row is red on both sides. **The honest verdict is that for a private Polish dental practice without an NFZ contract they fit better today, and the published post says so.** |

newly urgent: the vendors above advertise e-Recepta and EDM integration,
and the comparison pages will want to link it.

### A9 · Blocked on language — do not write these yet

Real vendors, real markets, and a post in a language we publish reaches
almost none of the people choosing between them and us. Move a row up
into its own section when the site publishes in that language.

**This section is empty.** The three German rows that lived here moved
into A6 when the site started publishing in German. The rule stays: a
vendor whose buyers search in a language we do not publish goes here, not
into a section it cannot rank in.

## Queue B — topics (the 16:00 UTC run)

Ordered by intent. Work down it; the top of this list is where a clinic
is closest to needing what we sell.

**These go out in every language the site publishes**, except where the
row says otherwise. The two regional variants are the exception that is not
worth a column: they inherit the parent's post, and a variant only gets its
own file when the market changes the answer (currency, tax, regulator). The problems are the same in Madrid, Lyon, Munich,
Milan and Manchester, and there is no competitor claim to re-verify per
copy.

The exception is the rows marked **country-locked**. Compliance is
national: the Spanish, French, German and Italian rows below are
different posts with different law, different deadlines and different
official sources, not translations of each other.

| Liquidar a los doctores colaboradores: lo que el software tiene que calcular | `liquidacion-doctores-colaboradores` / `associate-dentist-pay-calculation` / `retrocession-honoraires-collaborateur-dentaire` / `honorarios-dentistas-colaboradores` / `umsatzbeteiligung-angestellte-zahnaerzte` / `compenso-collaboratori-studio-odontoiatrico` | liquidación colaboradores clínica dental · Added 15 September 2026, intent verified that run in five markets, and the head term is different in every one: `rétrocession d'honoraires` is the contractual word in France (and the Ordre National des Chirurgiens-Dentistes publishes on its VAT thresholds), `compenso ai collaboratori` in Italy, `Umsatzbeteiligung` for an angestellter Zahnarzt in Germany, `rozliczanie lekarzy współpracujących` in Poland (Polish slug `rozliczanie-lekarzy-wspolpracujacych`). **Translating this row is the way to waste it.** The post is the arithmetic the software has to hold, not the percentage: which treatments are attributed to which practitioner, what is deducted before the percentage applies (laboratory and materials, and whether the deduction is gross or net), what happens to a treatment two practitioners touched, and what the monthly statement has to show so the practitioner can check it. **Publish no recommended percentage**: the figures circulating in each market come from consultants and trade press, not from an official source, so cite them as what a named source says, with its date, or leave them out. The tax and employment status of the collaborator is national, so name the national source or say plainly that the post does not cover it, and it is not legal or tax advice |
| Un paciente pide su historia clínica: qué entregar, en qué plazo y en qué formato | `paciente-pide-su-historia-clinica` / `patient-requests-their-dental-records` / `patient-demande-son-dossier-dentaire` / `paciente-pede-o-seu-processo-clinico` / `patient-fordert-patientenakte-an` / `paziente-chiede-la-cartella-clinica` | paciente pide historia clínica · Added 15 September 2026, intent verified that run in Spanish, German and Polish. RGPD art. 15 is the floor everywhere the site publishes, which is why this is not country-locked, but **the deadline, the fee and the medical-record layer on top are national and each post cites its own regulator**: AEPD and Ley 41/2002 in Spain, § 630g BGB and the Court of Justice ruling on the first copy being free in Germany, the ustawa o prawach pacjenta in Poland (14 days, first copy free since 4 May 2019, Polish slug `udostepnianie-dokumentacji-medycznej-pacjentowi`), CNIL and the ONCD in France. Re-source every deadline and fee the run it is written, never from another language's copy, and say plainly it is not legal advice. The software half is the useful half: what a complete export contains (notes, odontogram, images, quotes, consents), what format it comes out in, and how the request is logged |
| Traspaso de la clínica: qué pasa con las historias clínicas | `traspaso-clinica-dental-historias` / `dental-practice-sale-patient-records` / `cession-cabinet-dentaire-dossiers-patients` / `trespasse-clinica-dentaria-processos` / `praxisabgabe-patientenakten` / `cessione-studio-odontoiatrico-cartelle` | traspaso clínica dental pacientes · Added 15 September 2026, intent verified that run in Spanish, German and French. The clinic-side mirror of the `proveedor-software-cierra` row: there the vendor goes, here the practice changes hands and the records cannot simply move with the furniture. Germany publishes the most concrete model and it is worth describing rather than inventing one, the Zwei-Schrank-Modell that the data protection authorities set out for a Praxisübergabe, with the digital version being a locked predecessor archive the buyer opens per patient on consent; France frames it as cession de patientèle with CNIL rules on how the files travel; Spain as a change of controller under the RGPD. Source each from its own regulator or professional body the run it is written, and keep the post on what the buyer and seller have to be able to do in the software: separate the two record sets, keep the audit trail, export completely. Not legal advice |
| Cuánto hay que guardar una historia clínica dental y cómo destruirla después | `conservar-historia-clinica-dental-plazos` / `how-long-to-keep-dental-records` / `duree-conservation-dossier-dentaire` / `prazo-conservacao-processo-clinico-dentario` / `aufbewahrungsfristen-patientenakte-zahnarztpraxis` / `conservazione-cartelle-cliniche-odontoiatriche` | cuánto tiempo conservar historia clínica · Added 16 September 2026, intent verified that run in Spanish, German and Polish, and it is a different question in each: five years from the last asistencia in Spain, ten in Germany, twenty in Poland. The mirror of the `paciente-pide-su-historia-clinica` row and it must not repeat it: that one is about handing a copy out, this one is about how long the practice is obliged to hold the record and what it does when the clock runs out. **Each post cites its own primary text, never another language's copy and never the trade blogs that carry these figures**: Ley 41/2002 art. 17 plus the autonomous community's own rule in Spain (the plazos differ by comunidad and the post has to say so rather than publishing one number for the country), § 630f Abs. 3 BGB in Germany with the separate limitation periods of §§ 195-199 BGB as the reason practices keep longer, the ustawa o prawach pacjenta i Rzeczniku Praw Pacjenta art. 29 in Poland (Polish slug `okres-przechowywania-dokumentacji-medycznej-w-gabinecie-stomatologicznym`, and the exceptions are the interesting part: records of a child treated before its second birthday, analogue radiographs held outside the record, skierowania), the Code de la santé publique in France, and the national text for Portugal and Italy rather than the Spanish one. Radiographs, consents and casts can each carry their own clock, so state which. The software half is what the post is for: a retention period is unenforceable if nothing tells the practice which records have aged out, so cover what has to be searchable (date of the last entry, not the date the patient was created), what "destruir" means for a database and a backup that still holds the row, and what the practice keeps as evidence that it destroyed anything. Not legal advice |

| Protección radiológica en la clínica dental: qué registros hay que tener al día | `proteccion-radiologica-clinica-dental` / `dental-x-ray-radiation-protection-records` / `radioprotection-cabinet-dentaire` / `protecao-radiologica-clinica-dentaria` / `strahlenschutz-zahnarztpraxis` / `radioprotezione-studio-odontoiatrico` | protección radiológica clínica dental · Added 20 September 2026, intent verified that run in Spanish and German. Polish slug `ochrona-radiologiczna-w-gabinecie-stomatologicznym`. **Two published rows are adjacent and this one must not repeat either**: `almacenar-radiografias-digitales` is where the images live and `integrar-radiologia-software-dental` is the bridge to the imaging software. This row is the legal and record-keeping layer around the machine: the declaration or licence of the installation, the shielding report, the acceptance and constancy tests, the staff dosimetry and training record, the radiation-protection programme, and the justification of each radiograph inside the clinical record. **The authority and the text are national and each post cites its own**, never another language's copy. Two were verified this run and are safe to name: Spain is the CSN plus **Real Decreto 1085/2009, de 3 de julio, por el que se aprueba el Reglamento sobre instalación y utilización de aparatos de rayos X con fines de diagnóstico médico**, in force with its last update published 21 December 2022 per the BOE consolidated text, and the CSN publishes its own inspection actas for dental clinics; Germany is StrlSchG and StrlSchV (the every-one-to-three-years quality-assurance duty is anchored in § 86 Abs. 2 Nr. 9 StrlSchG with § 130 StrlSchV per the Landeszahnärztekammern) plus the **Zahnärztliche Stelle Röntgen** at the state chamber, which is the body a German practice actually deals with and which no other market has. France, Italy, Portugal and Poland were **not** verified this run: establish the authority and the text in force from the national regulator before writing, and if a run cannot, write the languages it can and leave the rest to the next one rather than guessing a decree number. The software half is the half that is ours to be useful about: a radiograph is only defensible if the record says who justified it, on what indication and with what exposure, so cover what has to be stored next to the image, what the retention clock is (it can differ from the record's own, see `conservar-historia-clinica-dental-plazos`) and what a practice can actually produce when an inspector asks for the last year of exposures. Not legal advice, and not a substitute for the UTPR, SPR or Zahnärztliche Stelle. **Written 21 September 2026 in all seven locales, and the four unverified authorities resolved.** Spain: RD 1085/2009 art. 17 classifies **dental intraoral as tipo 3 but dental NON-intraoral (panorámico, CBCT) as tipo 2**, and the type sets the calendar, so the certificado de conformidad by a UTPR/SPR is anual/bienal/quinquenal for types 1/2/3 (art. 18.e-f), types 1 and 2 send an annual or biennial informe to the CSN (art. 18.g) and type 3 instead keeps the same information as registros for at least ten years (art. 18.h); vigilancia de niveles de radiación is anual for everyone (art. 18.d), dosimetry is determined monthly (art. 19.3.b), and art. 19.4 requires three archives, dosimetry, radiological surveillance and staff training. The declaración goes to the comunidad autónoma before puesta en funcionamiento with three anexos, and failing to subsanar in 15 days obliges the titular to return the equipment to the supplier (art. 12). CSN acreditación has no validity period in the RD itself, so the post does not claim one. **France: régime de déclaration**, décision ASN 2018-DC-0649 under R.1333-109 2° and R.1333-110 CSP, récépissé with a CODEP and a SIGIS number is the act permitting the activity; formation à la radioprotection des patients is valid **ten years** for chirurgiens-dentistes; the maintenance and quality-control register of art. R.5212-28 CSP is kept **five years after the device leaves service**, not after the operation; vérification initiale by an accredited body at commissioning and important modification, renewed every three years only for interventional equipment; the compte rendu content is the arrêté du 22 septembre 2006 under R.1333-66 CSP. Source: the ASN guide *Principales dispositions réglementaires... en radiologie médicale et dentaire*, édition octobre 2024, served from the ASNR site. **Italy: notifica preventiva di pratica at least ten days before detention** (art. 46 c. 2 D.Lgs. 101/2020) to ASL, ITL, vigili del fuoco and ARPA, preceded by the nomina dell'ERP (art. 128) and accompanied by the relazione ex art. 109 c. 2. A dentist can be RIR only as esercente, or, if not esercente, only for endorali up to 70 kV. The centrepiece for us is **Allegato XXIX**: a registro informatico of every exam recording sesso, età and the tempo di scatto in seconds for endorali or the prodotto dose per area in mGy/cm² for OPT and cone beam. Sorveglianza fisica and controllo di qualità are at least annual; cessazione is notified 30 days ahead. Source: the INAIL/ASL Roma 6 guide *La pratica radiologica nel settore odontoiatrico*, July 2021. **Portugal: intraoral is registo, everything else is licença.** APA's own presentation lists 'Operação de equipamentos de medicina dentária intraoral' under registo (art. 4, art. 22), so an OPG or CBCT moves the clinic to licenciamento; art. 32 lists the six registo elements and art. 33 the licença additions; the RPR (art. 159) needs nível 1 or 2 under DL 227/2008, with the art. 207 transition closed in 2022; acceptance tests before first patient use per art. 104(3) against EC Radiation Protection 162; **portable intraoral units are explicitly desencorajados**; inspection is IGAMAOT on the mainland, DROTA in Madeira and IRA in the Açores, with regulation by APA except the Açores (DRA). **Poland: the permit comes from the państwowy wojewódzki inspektor sanitarny**, not the PAA (art. 4 ust. 1 pkt 10-11 and art. 5 ust. 4 Prawa atomowego), and intraoral units are exempt from the projekt osłon stałych and from the IOR opinion in that application but not from the permit itself. The two regulations that make the post are Dz.U. 2023 poz. 195 (§ 3 ust. 3: no skierowanie for intraoral; § 3 ust. 4: **the DICOM obligation does not apply to intraoral**; § 5: exposure parameters go in the dokumentacja medyczna except for intraoral, **where a record that the examination was performed is still required**; 60-70 kV, rectangular collimation, thyroid shielding) and Dz.U. 2022 poz. 2759 (testy podstawowe co miesiąc and co 6 miesięcy; testy specjalistyczne **every 24 months for intraoral and every 12 for pantomograficzne and cefalometryczne**). Both Polish acts were fetched from dziennikustaw.gov.pl because ISAP serves a CAPTCHA. The English copy is anchored on the UK (IRR17 SI 2017/1075 and IR(ME)R17 SI 2017/1322) and says plainly that the answer is national: HSE registration before first use, the identity of the IR(ME)R practitioner recorded for every exposure, exposure factors recorded only on deviation from a guideline protocol, routine testing normally at intervals not exceeding three years but annual for hand-held sets, procedures reviewed at intervals not exceeding three years, staff training records at least five years, and **IR(ME)R specifies no retention period at all**, with the guidance advising five years. Source: the PHE/FGDP *Guidance Notes for Dental Practitioners on the Safe Use of X-ray Equipment*, 2nd edition, 2020. |
| La inspección sanitaria llega a la clínica: qué tiene que poder enseñar ese día | `inspeccion-sanitaria-clinica-dental` / `dental-practice-inspection-readiness` / `inspection-sanitaire-cabinet-dentaire` / `inspecao-sanitaria-clinica-dentaria` / `praxisbegehung-zahnarztpraxis` / `ispezione-sanitaria-studio-odontoiatrico` | inspección sanitaria clínica dental · Added 20 September 2026, intent verified that run in Spanish, German and Polish, and **the query is a different question in each, which is the whole reason this is not one post translated seven times**. Germany searches `Praxisbegehung Zahnarztpraxis` and the competing pages are chamber checklists (the Zahnärztekammer Berlin publishes a *Checkliste Praxisbegehung*, the KVSH another) plus hygiene suppliers, so the German copy is organised around the Begehung by the Gesundheitsamt and the hygiene, Medizinprodukte-Aufbereitung and QM documentation it turns on. Poland searches `kontrola sanepidu w gabinecie stomatologicznym` (Polish slug `kontrola-sanepidu-w-gabinecie-stomatologicznym`) and the competing pages are trade press, so that copy is organised around the Państwowa Inspekcja Sanitarna, the procedury przeciwepidemiczne, the sterilisation register and the staff sanitary-epidemiological examinations. Spain is not an announced Begehung at all but the autonomic **autorización sanitaria de funcionamiento** under Real Decreto 1277/2003 and its renewal, with an on-site inspection: the Comunidad de Madrid publishes a *Lista de verificación para clínicas dentales* (Dirección General de Inspección y Ordenación Sanitaria, asset dated 31 March 2025) and the Basque Government publishes the procedure including the inspection visit and the five-year renewal, so the Spanish copy is built on the autonomous community's own checklist and says plainly that the requirements differ by comunidad rather than publishing one national answer. Source each country from its own regulator or chamber the run it is written, never from another language's copy and never from the hygiene suppliers that dominate these results. **Four published rows are adjacent and this one links rather than repeats them**: `trazabilidad-esterilizacion-dental`, `auditoria-accesos-historia-clinica`, `rgpd-clinica-dental` and `abrir-clinica-dental-software`, plus the radiation row above. What makes it a guide and not a checklist reprint is the software half: which of the things an inspector asks for are records a practice can only produce if something was logging them all year, what has to be searchable on the day (sterilisation cycles by date, consents by patient, exposures by equipment, access log by user), and what is a paper folder that no software will save. Not legal advice  **Written 22 September 2026 in all seven locales, and the four authorities the row left open are now established.** **Spain**: the frame is not a Begehung but the autonomic autorización, RD 1277/2003 (tipología C.2.5.1, unidades U.44/U.45/U.35) plus Decreto 51/2006 and Orden 1158/2018 in Madrid; the Madrid checklist (ed. 31/03/2025) gives gabinete 8 m2 destinado en exclusiva, structure must match the filed planos, colegiación certificate valid at most 6 months, director asistencial on the plantilla, Orden de Visibilidad and plantilla displayed, and renovacion every five or eight years with three months notice (Euskadi: five years, with an inspection visit). New and worth carrying: **Real Decreto 239/2026, BOE 26 March 2026, in force 1 July 2026**, adds art. 7 Garantias de atencion sanitaria, an up-to-date staff-documentation duty. **France** is two controls, not one, and that is the whole shape of the post: ARS inspection of the risque infectieux (ARS Paca: dental activity in the inspection programme since 2015, suspension immediate of a centre or of a liberal practitioner for a maximum of five months, 2 and 8 such suspensions in 2025) plus the DGCCRF control of information des patients that ONCD announced on 14 January 2025 (tarifs, devis, 100% sante cosignes, tracabilite des DM). ARS Paca's September 2024 fiches give the marche en avant and the zone sale/propre layout; ONCD gives the arrete du 30 mai 2018 affichage and the 70 euro devis threshold. sante.gouv.fr is unreachable from the runner, so the DGS grille technique of 2011 could not be sourced and is not cited. **Italy**: autorizzazione is regional on top of art. 8-ter c. 2 D.Lgs. 502/1992 (required 'ove attrezzati per erogare prestazioni di chirurgia ambulatoriale'); Toscana Regolamento 61/R del 24 dicembre 2010 Allegato C is the worked example and the quotable one, with 'Le prestazioni effettuate devono essere registrate con completezza ed accuratezza e corredate dalle generalita riferite all'utente', the inventario delle apparecchiature, the documento sui guasti and the documented modalita di compilazione, conservazione, archiviazione dei documenti. **Portugal**: Portaria n.o 99/2024/1 de 13 de marco, in force the day after publication, revoking Portarias 268/2010 and 167-A/2014; licensing by mera comunicacao previa under art. 4.o n.o 4 a) DL 127/2014; art. 7.o splits processos clinicos from the five-year set, art. 8.o is the always-accessible documentation, art. 10.o n.o 5 allows one direcao clinica per clinic except single-professional consultorios. The ERS publishes its actual fiscalizacao form, Mod.133_02 (2024), which is the post's spine, and the SRER certidao must be afixada in a public place. **English copy is anchored on England and says so**: CQC registration under the Health and Social Care Act 2008 and SI 2014/2936, regs 12 and 17 the most breached, reg 17(2)(c) the contemporaneous-record duty, and the provider information request answered in five working days (statement of purpose, staff by role and hours, complaints with actions). CQC's dental guidance pages carry an under-review notice after the 2024 change of approach, and the post states that. Retrieval notes for the next run: cqc.org.uk, salute.gov.it and regione.lombardia.it refuse a plain fetch or 404, and CQC needs a browser user agent; sante.gouv.fr returns an empty reply entirely. PDFs from lzk-bw, zaek-berlin, comunidad.madrid, ers.pt and diariodarepublica need local text extraction, pdfminer.six in a venv, because the fetch tool returns raw PDF. |

Polish slug `umowa-powierzenia-przetwarzania-danych-w-gabinecie-stomatologicznym`. Not country-locked, because **art. 28 RGPD is the same floor everywhere the site publishes**, but each post cites its own authority on top: verified this run, the AEPD publishes both a model clause set for processor contracts (`aepd.es/documento/clausulas-contratos-encargado-tratamiento.pdf`) and an FAQ on the contract's required content, and the Landeszahnärztekammer Baden-Württemberg publishes a whole *Kapitel 2 - Auftragsverarbeitung* for practices; CNIL, the Garante, UODO and the CNPD are the counterparts and are established by the run that writes those copies. **Three published rows are adjacent and this one must not repeat them**: `rgpd-clinica-dental` is the general obligation, `preguntas-antes-de-firmar-software-dental` is the commercial due diligence, and `self-hosting-con-tu-informatico` is the SLA with the integrator. This row is the one contract, clause by clause: the object and duration, the instruction-only limit, confidentiality, the security measures annex, **the sub-processor list and the right to object to a new one**, assistance with patient rights requests, audit rights, and what happens to the data at the end of the contract. The search results in Spanish are consultancies and one competitor's own blog, which is exactly the gap. Two things make it useful rather than a template reprint and both are ours to say honestly: an AI or analytics clause that licenses patient data for model training is a processor term a practice can refuse, and it is published in the open by at least one vendor already in Queue A (see the Practice-Web row in A3), so the post can describe the shape of such a clause without naming anyone; and self-hosting does not remove the contract, it moves it, because the backup, the remote support session and the hosting provider are each a processor even when the database sits in the clinic. State what a practice actually has to hold, and say plainly it is not legal advice |

Establish each country's frame from its own chamber, council or regulator the run it is written, never from another language's copy. **Four published rows are adjacent and this one links rather than repeats them**: `enviar-casos-laboratorio-dental` is a supplier and not a colleague, `integrar-radiologia-software-dental` is the imaging bridge, `paciente-pide-su-historia-clinica` is the patient's own art. 15 request, and `traspaso-clinica-dental-historias` is the practice changing hands. The post is what travels and what comes back: the clinical question actually being asked, the history and the medical alerts, which radiographs and with what dates, what has already been tried, and the lawful basis and the patient's knowledge of the transfer, because the receiving practice is another controller and not a processor. Then the software half, which is the useful half and which no form template covers: how the referral is recorded so the next person opening the record sees it, how images leave without becoming email attachments, **how the practice finds out that a referral never came back**, and what the access log shows afterwards. When to refer is a clinical judgement and is not this post; any clinical content is sourced from a professional body or a peer-reviewed source with the date consulted, and the post says plainly it is neither legal nor clinical advice |

In Spanish the clinic partners with a financiera that lends to the patient and pays the clinic; in German the dominant instrument is **Factoring**, the practice selling the patient invoice on and the provider taking over invoicing, Mahnwesen and the patient's Ratenzahlung. Three vendors already in Queue A publish on this in Spanish (Odontonet, Dentalink, Klinikare) and the German field has its own specialised providers: that is the commercial-intent signal, and **none of those pages is a source**. Each locale then carries its own national anchor: ES Ley 16/2011 arts. 3.c, 3.f, 4.3, 26 and 29; EN the RAO 2001 arts. 36A and 36B, FSMA 2000 s. 19 and CCA 1974 s. 75 (the creditor is indemnified by the supplier, which is the whole point); DE § 203 StGB, KWG § 1(1a) no. 9 and § 32, GewO § 34c; IT TUB arts. 121, 125-quinquies and 128-quater; PL ustawa o kredycie konsumenckim arts. 3, 5, 56, 57 and 59. **FR and PT rest on the directive alone**: legifrance.gouv.fr and diariodarepublica.pt both refuse automated fetches (403 and a JS bot wall), so no national article number was asserted for those two. German is the better half of the post and has real primary sources: the Bundeszahnärztekammer and several Landeszahnärztekammern publish position papers on the **Ausfallhonorar**, and they condition it — a Bestellpraxis with the slot reserved for that patient alone, explicit prior notice that is not buried in small print, no fault on the patient's side, the slot not refilled, and the loss calculated concretely and evidenced rather than charged as a flat fee. **Whether anything can be charged at all is national and conditioned**, so each language sources its own basis (dental chamber, professional body or consumer law) with the date consulted, and says plainly it is not legal advice. In Spain it is the five-year minimum of Ley 41/2002 art. 17 plus the **bloqueo** of LOPDGDD art. 32, sourced from the AEPD directly (its *Tus derechos en relación con tus datos de salud* page and Informe 0049/2005) and the APDCAT patient guide.
